The accepted K6-003 callback moved only the sole self-header directive from UID00018E to the beginning of UID00050J, then validated both pages and physically proved the generated first-directive/global/specialization/router/aggregate order. Supervisor IDA, coverage verification, and independent validation claims remain unchecked; execution/archive authority is lifecycle-neutral in ordinary prose and is established only by the report's current path plus validator-owned status/history metadata.

## Executive Recommendation

- Keep InputMan as a narrow standalone input/IMM module under `NexusTK/input/`.
- Do not move Event construction bodies, IME pane rendering, List internals, MemoryMan, or PlatformApi dispatch storage into this file.
- Treat the ten no-route IMM forwarding bodies as retained source-authored file-local wrappers, not import thunks: their framed tail jumps are inside the InputMan source island, while the actual IAT thunks are at `0x005c5cxx`.
- Treat jump tables, value table, padding, the `0x004e9650` constructor-unwind Singleton cleanup, scalar deleting destructor, RTTI, and vtable bytes as compiler-covered/no-code.
- Reclassify UID0003BR to non-reconstructable/non-emitting with class UID00006J as semantic owner; the virtual destructor plus direct `LObject` and `Singleton<InputMan>` inheritance regenerate the complete RTTI hierarchy and all three vtable slots.

## Inference Research Guidance Check

- IDA fact, documentation evidence, and inference are separated throughout.
- Existing descriptive names were not presumed original. Names such as `GetImeStateByte`, `GetKeyboardLayoutLanguageName`, `GetCurrentImeContext`, and the ten wrapper names remain descriptive.
- The old generated `g_MessageCallback` claim is rejected: `0x004e8cb0` is called with `ECX=this` and five stack arguments and returns with `retn 14h`, matching a non-static InputMan fallback handler.
- The old free-factory interpretation for `0x004a9a40-0x004a9f00` is rejected at the call sites because `ECX` is explicitly loaded with `g_pEventMan` before each call.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

| Issue | Reanalysis and disposition |
| --- | --- |
| Class bases | Constructor calls `LObject` construction and then lowers a second direct empty base at `this+4`; destructor order clears the singleton before `LObject` teardown. InputMan COL `0x00649444` points to CHD `0x00649458` with attributes `1`, base count `3`, and descriptors for InputMan PMD 0, LObject PMD 0, and `Singleton<InputMan>` PMD +4. Use `class InputMan : public LObject, public Singleton<InputMan>`. |
| Singleton shape | Constructor instructions `lea ebx,[edi+4]`, `lea eax,[ebx-4]`, and the publish store prove empty-base downcast lowering for `Singleton<InputMan>::Singleton()`. Ordinary/scalar teardown clears immediately before `LObject::~LObject`, proving reverse-base `Singleton<InputMan>::~Singleton()` lowering. Emit explicit class-specific specializations beside `g_pInputMan`; emit no manual publication/clear in the derived bodies. |
| `+0x0c` field | Destructor reads and conditionally associates it, but constructor never writes it. Preserve the likely original uninitialized-field defect by omitting its constructor initializer. |
| `0x004e8cb0` | One WndProc caller sets `ECX` to InputMan and supplies the same five explicit arguments as the primary handler. Descriptive member name `HandleDefaultWindowMessage`; return is always false. |
| `TrackInputTarget` return | Six callers ignore `AL`; the body has no intentional return assignment. Recommend `void`, not the current forced `true`. |
| WM_USER+1 source name | The binary proves the message value and empty/non-empty target transition, not an original symbol. The formal source's internally consistent context-notification role is the strongest human name, so use `kInputManContextMessage` for the declaration and every use. |
| Event calls | Disassembly loads `g_pEventMan` into `ECX` before all eight InputMan event calls. Add member declarations to EventMan H support; do not retain local free prototypes. |
| WM_INPUTLANGCHANGE | Binary recreates the owned context, tests an app-window context, conditionally associates the new context, writes no result, and returns false. It does not use the message HWND or close/release the tested context. |
| WM_CHAR | Binary optionally dispatches when bit `0x20000000` is clear, then always writes zero and returns true. |
| Composition result slot | WM_IME_COMPOSITION returns true without writing `*result`; current CPP writes zero. |
| Candidate allocator | Raw `CANDIDATELIST` storage uses `MemoryMan::AllocateBufferMemory`/`FreeBufferMemory`, not operator new/delete. |
| Candidate encoding | Style bit 2 plus count 1 uses one/two packed bytes and `MultiByteToWideChar`; normal offsets already address UTF-16 strings and must not be ANSI-converted. |
| Candidate page start | The special packed-candidate branch reaches dispatch without initializing the stack local later used as page start. Preserve that observed original defect rather than silently setting it to zero. |
| Candidate deque lifetime | InputMan heap-allocates it; type-15 dispatch is synchronous; pane code copies it; Event destruction and dispatch cleanup do not delete it. Preserve the observed leak and document it. |
| Raw/no-route wrappers | Exact framed tail jumps and source-island placement support retained file-local wrapper bodies. Their original accessibility and spellings are not proven; file-local descriptive names are the conservative source shape. |
| `0x004e9650` | Two instructions clear `g_pInputMan` and return; the sole incoming xref is the constructor EH action at `0x0060063e`, which passes the already-constructed `this+4` Singleton facet. This is compiler-retained unwind lowering of `Singleton<InputMan>::~Singleton()`, not a hand-authored helper; formal CPP remains blank for this range. |
| Scalar destructor/vtable/tables | Compiler-generated; no hand-authored source body or raw bytes. |
| UID00006J emitter route | Historical failed-gate state had a blank managed CPP block and only a prose `[[CHILDREN]]` occurrence, so validator semantics emitted an Empty Emitter Marker. The accepted callback applied the minimal deterministic repair while keeping UID00006J's direct-file route/position and UID00018E's sole child route: one formal `[[CHILDREN]]` line is now consumed by recursive assembly, emits UID00018E exactly once, and leaves the independent H channel unchanged. Historicalized command `000000027608` and historical dated command `000000027614` both confirm the resolved route; future review must reread dynamically. |
| Translation-unit declaration order | Direct emitter position `0` places UID00050J before the UID00006J/UID00018E expansion. A pointer definition alone could use a forward declaration, but the explicit `Singleton<InputMan>` specializations and `static_cast<InputMan *>(this)` require the complete class/template relationship. Accepted K6-003 moved the sole self-header to UID00050J first and removed its UID00018E copy while retaining Event/List/MemoryMan/STL includes. Callback command `000000027648` established the exact corrected order, and later live command `000000027668` retains it. |

Rejected alternatives include merging with Application, moving candidate production into IMEPanes, treating normal candidate strings as ACP bytes, inventing a candidate deque deleter, reconstructing STL helper `0x004e9710`, hand-authoring vtable bytes, and treating the wrapper band as padding/import-thunk ownership.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- One constructor caller and one primary WndProc caller establish application lifetime and message entry.
- Six target-tracking callers and exact empty/non-empty transitions establish the List role.
- Exact vtable stores, inherited slots, destructor route, and `0x20` layout establish the class declaration.
- COL/CHD/base-descriptor bytes and the preserved executable's decorated Singleton token prove direct `LObject` plus `Singleton<InputMan>` inheritance independently of constructor-shape inference.
- Constructor `this+4` downcast publication, reverse ordinary/scalar clearing, and the EH-only clear action match the established project pattern of two explicit class-specific Singleton specializations with no manual derived-body assignments.
- Exact IMM imports, PlatformApi slots, EventMan receiver loads, and IMEPanes consumer establish dependency direction.
- Raw helper and wrapper boundaries are contiguous with the InputMan island and separated by deterministic `0xcc` alignment.
- Current route metadata forms one acyclic chain: UID00050J -> UID0000K6 at position 0; UID00006J -> UID0000K6 at position 1; UID00018E -> UID00006J as its sole emitter. `by-structure.md` and validator semantics prove that placing `[[CHILDREN]]` inside UID00006J's managed CPP block consumes the marker and recursively expands UID00018E once, while the independent nonblank H continues to create `InputMan.h`. The same direct-position evidence proves why the self-header must be emitted by UID00050J rather than deferred to UID00018E; route completeness alone is not compile completeness.
- Dated isolated Gate 2B plus B008 read-only follow-up prove P026's complete persistence path: initial unsaved analysis materializes `_DWORD *` to `LObject *`; after pure rename the item/name/disassembly layers use `InputMan__ScalarDeletingDestructor` while same-session Hex-Rays can retain `sub_4E9660`; after the sole save, fresh reopen retains `LObject *` and Hex-Rays uses the persisted renamed declaration. Frame, comment channels, extent, and vtable slot remain deterministic.
- Current canonical exact-name evidence cleanly separates two IDA layers: `inspect_items` shows each expected old/default display label at its exact target, while anchored `entity_query(kind:"names")` proves that thirteen autogenerated labels have no registered old-name row and A026 alone has one registered old-name row. All fourteen desired names are absent at baseline, so every no-overwrite rename has a deterministic observed registry prestate without inventing an old registration.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

### 1. UID0000K6 InputMan

- Evidence for: contiguous InputMan class/helper island, unique class/vtable/global lifetime, dedicated current path, application construction, and subsystem-specific dependencies.
- Evidence against: exact historical spelling of the directory and no-route wrappers is unavailable.
- Decision: accepted sole file root.

### 2. Application

- Evidence for: constructs, destroys, supplies window, and calls handlers.
- Evidence against: no implementation bodies, state, vtable, or singleton storage belong to Application; dependency direction is consumer/lifetime only.
- Decision: rejected as source owner.

### 3. Event or IMEPanes

- Evidence for: EventMan receives translated events; IMEPanes consumes composition/candidate data.
- Evidence against: Event owns record construction/dispatch, IMEPanes owns display panes, while InputMan owns Win32/IMM translation and context state.
- Decision: support dependencies only.

### Proposed new file/grouping, if applicable

- Not applicable. No split or new grouping is justified.

## Source Placement

- Recommended: `NexusTK/input/InputMan.cpp` and `NexusTK/input/InputMan.h`.
- The input path matches IMEPanes adjacency and the file's full Win32/IMM role.
- Exact output route: UID00050J direct child position `0`; UID00006J direct child position `1`; UID00018E sole child of UID00006J; UID00006J managed CPP exactly `[[CHILDREN]]`; UID00006J managed H unchanged. UID00050J formal CPP now begins with the sole `#include "InputMan.h"`, then emits the global and two specializations; recursive CPP assembly emits UID00006J/UID00018E once, with Event/List/MemoryMan/STL includes retained at the aggregate start and no second self-header. H assembly emits only the complete class declaration.
- Reject `Application.cpp`, `ui/core/Event.cpp`, `input/IMEPanes.cpp`, standalone generated wrapper files, and a handwritten CandidateStringQueue file.
- Exact historical capitalization is not proven, but current project style consistently uses `InputMan`.

Each NR pair has exact entity-level acceptance states: the initial/pre-action and post-dry state reproduces thirteen `0/0` pairs plus A026 `1/0` with the old display independently proven; the post-persistent and persisted-state readback has old `total:0,data:[]` and desired `total:1` with one sole ordered `{kind:"name",addr:"<exact target>",name:"<exact desired>"}` row and no duplicate or off-target row. For every old and desired query, result `error` may be absent or null and must never be nonempty; `next_offset` may be absent or null and must never be non-null. Any extra/reordered row, present nonempty error, present non-null continuation, total/address/name/kind mismatch, or display/range drift hard-stops entity acceptance. One complete registry state readback is fourteen pairs / twenty-eight anchored subqueries; these reads add no persistent or dry-run actions.

For every protection row, preflight and final readback must use independent `get_bytes`, `inspect_items`, `get_comments`, `stack_frame`, `xref_query`, exact anchored NR `entity_query(kind:"names")`, decompilation/declaration, function-chunk, callee, raw-disassembly, and neighbor reads. `lookup_funcs("sub_<address>")` may be retained only as address/display behavior and receives no registered-name credit. Function rename/comment actions authorize only the named display/declaration and exact NR registry transition. The two raw address-comment actions authorize only their exact address-regular channel; raw function/name/type/frame/catalog creation is prohibited. Bytes, hashes, code/data xrefs, frames, item heads, boundaries, unrelated registered names, chunks, raw instructions, and neighbors are never authorized to change. Every type endpoint is forbidden. P026 alone has the exact observed analysis-materialization/persistence contract: initial S0-S2 `_DWORD *`, S3-S8 and T0-T3 `LObject *`, then persisted-state T4-T7 `LObject *`; this persistence occurs without `set_type` and permits no other type delta or reset action.

Raw preservation order is fixed: preflight the matching protection/RAW row; require its exact no-function/name/type/declaration/frame state and zero frame rows; perform no function creation or rename; apply only A040 or A047 when their exact address-comment dependency is reached; then independently reread catalog/range/bytes/hash/heads/comments/frame/xrefs/neighbors. Any raw catalog, item-kind, function, name, type, declaration, frame, byte, hash, boundary, xref, or neighbor delta is a hard stop.

Action accounting is exact: the table below contains 29 persistent supervisor-owned action rows, of which fourteen are pure modeled-function renames and fifteen are comment actions. Each rename row contains one additional read-only dry-run gate that is not a persistent action row. The complete rename response allowlist applies independently to every one of the fourteen actions. Dry and persistent summaries require exact `total:1`, `ok:1`, and `failed:0`; summary `stop_on_error` may be absent or must be exactly Boolean true, and every successful summary must physically omit `stopped_at`. At every dry/persistent summary and sole `func[0]` row scope, `stopped` and `allow_overwrite` may be absent or must be exactly Boolean false. Dry summary and sole row require Boolean-true `dry_run`; persistent summary and sole row permit `dry_run` absent or exactly Boolean false. Each sole row must contain the row-specific exact semantic-target `addr`, exact `old`, and exact `name`; `dir`, `func_addr`, and `new` must be physically absent; row `error` and `dir_error` may be absent or exactly empty string. Reject false/null/non-Boolean or contradictory `stop_on_error`, present `stopped_at`, true/null/non-Boolean `stopped` or `allow_overwrite`, missing/non-true dry `dry_run`, true/null/non-Boolean persistent `dry_run`, required-field mismatch, any forbidden row field, or any nonempty/non-string row error. Every request remains explicit for `dry_run`, `pure:true`, `allow_overwrite:false`, and `stop_on_error:true`. Each action's own row supplies its exact prestate, zero-delta dry safety check, expected name-registry transition, cumulative protected entity state, and hard stop. P026's entity-level acceptance classes are initial S0-S8, same-session T0-T3, and persisted-state T4-T7; no type endpoint or cold reset is part of any class. Any registry/display/result-row/summary/error/collision/overwrite/count/stage/type/name/frame/catalog or unrelated delta fails that entity action. Operational ordering and persistence handling remain solely within the external prerequisite above.

Exact replacement for UID00018E `RECONSTRUCTION_CPP CODE`:

```cpp
#include "../ui/core/Event.h"
#include "../util/List.h"
#include "../util/MemoryMan.h"

#include <deque>
#include <string>
#include <cstring>

extern HWND GetApplicationWindow();

extern LRESULT (WINAPI *g_pfnDefWindowProcW)(HWND, UINT, WPARAM, LPARAM);
extern BOOL (WINAPI *g_pfnPostMessageW)(HWND, UINT, WPARAM, LPARAM);
extern LONG (WINAPI *g_pfnImmGetCompositionStringW)(HIMC, DWORD, LPVOID, DWORD);
extern DWORD (WINAPI *g_pfnImmGetCandidateListW)(HIMC, DWORD, LPCANDIDATELIST, DWORD);
extern int (WINAPI *g_pfnGetLocaleInfoW)(LCID, LCTYPE, LPWSTR, int);

static const UINT kInputManContextMessage = WM_USER + 1;
static const DWORD kDefaultCandidatePageSize = 9;

static int __stdcall CopyCompositionString(DWORD flag, wchar_t *buffer,
                                           int byteCapacity);
static BOOL __stdcall ReleaseCurrentImeContext(HIMC context);

InputMan::InputMan()
{
    HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
    m_defaultImeWindow = ImmGetDefaultIMEWnd(appWindow);
    m_ownedImeContext = ImmCreateContext();
    ImmAssociateContext(appWindow, m_ownedImeContext);

    HIMC context = ImmGetContext(GetApplicationWindow());
    ImmSetOpenStatus(context, FALSE);
    ImmReleaseContext(GetApplicationWindow(), context);

    m_imePropertyFlags = ImmGetProperty(GetKeyboardLayout(0), IGP_PROPERTY);
    m_imeStateWord = 0;
    m_inputMode = 2;
    m_inputTargetList = new List(sizeof(int), 100);
}

InputMan::~InputMan()
{
    if (m_restoreImeContext != 0) {
        ImmAssociateContext(GetApplicationWindow(), m_restoreImeContext);
    }
    if (m_ownedImeContext != 0) {
        ImmDestroyContext(m_ownedImeContext);
    }
    if (m_inputTargetList != 0) {
        m_inputTargetList->Release(1);
    }
}

bool InputMan::HandleDefaultWindowMessage(HWND, UINT, WPARAM, LPARAM,
                                          LRESULT *)
{
    return false;
}

static int __stdcall GetKeyboardLayoutLanguageName(LPWSTR buffer,
                                                    int cchBuffer)
{
    HKL layout = GetKeyboardLayout(0);
    return g_pfnGetLocaleInfoW(LOWORD(layout), LOCALE_SLANGUAGE,
                               buffer, cchBuffer);
}

void InputMan::TrackInputTarget(int targetId, bool active)
{
    HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
    if (targetId == 0) {
        g_pfnPostMessageW(appWindow, kInputManContextMessage,
                          active ? 1 : 0, 0);
        m_inputTargetList->RemoveAll();
        return;
    }

    int oldCount = m_inputTargetList->GetCount();
    int foundIndex = -1;
    int *targets = static_cast<int *>(m_inputTargetList->GetData());
    for (int i = 0; i < oldCount; ++i) {
        if (targets[i] == targetId) {
            foundIndex = i;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (active) {
        if (foundIndex < 0) {
            m_inputTargetList->Append(1, &targetId);
        }
    } else if (foundIndex >= 0) {
        m_inputTargetList->RemoveAt(foundIndex, 1);
    }

    int newCount = m_inputTargetList->GetCount();
    if (oldCount == 0 && newCount != 0) {
        g_pfnPostMessageW(appWindow, kInputManContextMessage, 1, 0);
    } else if (oldCount != 0 && newCount == 0) {
        g_pfnPostMessageW(appWindow, kInputManContextMessage, 0, 0);
    }
}

unsigned char InputMan::GetImeStateByte() const
{
    return static_cast<unsigned char>(m_imeStateWord >> 8);
}

bool InputMan::HandleWindowMessage(HWND window, UINT message, WPARAM wParam,
                                   LPARAM lParam, LRESULT *result)
{
    switch (message) {
    case WM_INPUTLANGCHANGE: {
        if (m_ownedImeContext != 0) {
            ImmDestroyContext(m_ownedImeContext);
        }
        m_ownedImeContext = ImmCreateContext();
        HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
        if (ImmGetContext(appWindow) != 0) {
            ImmAssociateContext(appWindow, m_ownedImeContext);
        }
        return false;
    }

    case WM_CHAR:
        if ((lParam & 0x20000000) == 0) {
            g_pEventMan->DispatchCharEvent(static_cast<wchar_t>(wParam),
                                           GetMessageTime());
        }
        *result = 0;
        return true;

    case WM_IME_STARTCOMPOSITION:
        g_pEventMan->DispatchCompositionStartEvent(GetMessageTime());
        *result = 0;
        return true;

    case WM_IME_ENDCOMPOSITION:
        g_pEventMan->DispatchCompositionEndEvent(GetMessageTime());
        return false;

    case WM_IME_COMPOSITION: {
        wchar_t text[128];
        if ((lParam & GCS_RESULTSTR) != 0) {
            CopyCompositionString(GCS_RESULTSTR, text, sizeof(text));
            g_pEventMan->DispatchCompositionResultEvent(text,
                                                        GetMessageTime());
        }
        if ((lParam & GCS_COMPSTR) != 0) {
            CopyCompositionString(GCS_COMPSTR, text, 128);
            g_pEventMan->DispatchCompositionTextEvent(text,
                                                      GetMessageTime());
        }
        return true;
    }

    case WM_IME_SETCONTEXT:
        if ((m_imePropertyFlags & 0x20000) == 0) {
            *result = g_pfnDefWindowProcW(window, message, wParam, 0);
            return true;
        }
        return false;

    case WM_IME_NOTIFY:
        switch (wParam) {
        case IMN_OPENSTATUSWINDOW:
            *result = 0;
            return true;

        case IMN_CHANGECANDIDATE:
        case IMN_OPENCANDIDATE: {
            std::deque<std::wstring> *visibleCandidates =
                new std::deque<std::wstring>;

            DWORD listIndex = 0;
            DWORD listBit = 1;
            while ((static_cast<DWORD>(lParam) & listBit) == 0 &&
                   listIndex < 32) {
                ++listIndex;
                listBit = (listBit << 1) | (listBit >> 31);
            }

            HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
            HIMC context = ImmGetContext(appWindow);
            DWORD byteCount = g_pfnImmGetCandidateListW(context, listIndex,
                                                        0, 0);
            CANDIDATELIST *candidateList =
                static_cast<CANDIDATELIST *>(
                    GetMemoryMan()->AllocateBufferMemory(byteCount));
            g_pfnImmGetCandidateListW(context, listIndex, candidateList,
                                      byteCount);
            ReleaseCurrentImeContext(context);

            DWORD pageStart;
            if ((candidateList->dwStyle & 2) != 0 &&
                candidateList->dwCount == 1) {
                const char *packed = reinterpret_cast<const char *>(
                    &candidateList->dwOffset[0]);
                char multibyte[3];
                if (static_cast<signed char>(packed[0]) < 0) {
                    multibyte[0] = packed[1];
                    multibyte[1] = packed[0];
                    multibyte[2] = 0;
                } else {
                    multibyte[0] = packed[0];
                    multibyte[1] = 0;
                }

                wchar_t wideText[256];
                int length = MultiByteToWideChar(
                    CP_ACP, 0, multibyte,
                    static_cast<int>(std::strlen(multibyte)),
                    wideText, 256);
                wideText[length] = 0;
                visibleCandidates->push_back(std::wstring(wideText));
            } else {
                DWORD pageSize = candidateList->dwPageSize;
                if (pageSize == 0) {
                    pageSize = kDefaultCandidatePageSize;
                }
                pageStart = candidateList->dwPageStart;
                if (pageStart == 0) {
                    pageStart = pageSize *
                        (candidateList->dwSelection / pageSize);
                }

                DWORD pageEnd = pageStart + pageSize - 1;
                if (pageEnd > candidateList->dwCount - 1) {
                    pageEnd = candidateList->dwCount - 1;
                }
                for (DWORD i = pageStart; i <= pageEnd; ++i) {
                    const wchar_t *candidate =
                        reinterpret_cast<const wchar_t *>(
                            reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char *>(
                                candidateList) + candidateList->dwOffset[i]);
                    visibleCandidates->push_back(std::wstring(candidate));
                }
            }

            g_pEventMan->DispatchCandidateListEvent(
                static_cast<int>(candidateList->dwSelection - pageStart),
                visibleCandidates, GetMessageTime());
            GetMemoryMan()->FreeBufferMemory(candidateList);
            *result = 0;
            return true;
        }

        case IMN_CLOSECANDIDATE:
            g_pEventMan->DispatchCandidateCloseEvent(GetMessageTime());
            return false;

        case IMN_SETOPENSTATUS: {
            HIMC context = ImmGetContext(GetApplicationWindow());
            BOOL isOpen = ImmGetOpenStatus(context);
            ReleaseCurrentImeContext(context);
            g_pEventMan->DispatchImeOpenStatusEvent(isOpen,
                                                    GetMessageTime());
            *result = 0;
            return true;
        }
        }
        return false;

    case WM_IME_CHAR:
        *result = 0;
        return true;

    case WM_IME_KEYDOWN:
        *result = 0;
        return false;

    case kInputManContextMessage:
        ImmAssociateContext(GetApplicationWindow(),
                            LOBYTE(wParam) == 1 ? m_ownedImeContext : 0);
        *result = 0;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

int InputMan::GetInputMode() const
{
    return m_inputMode;
}

BOOL InputMan::SetInputMode(int mode)
{
    m_inputMode = mode;
    if (mode == 2) {
        HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
        HIMC context = ImmGetContext(appWindow);
        ImmNotifyIME(context, NI_COMPOSITIONSTR, CPS_COMPLETE, 0);
        return ImmReleaseContext(appWindow, context);
    }
    return mode;
}

BOOL __stdcall CompleteImeComposition()
{
    HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
    HIMC context = ImmGetContext(appWindow);
    ImmNotifyIME(context, NI_COMPOSITIONSTR, CPS_COMPLETE, 0);
    return ImmReleaseContext(appWindow, context);
}

int __stdcall GetCompositionCursorPosition()
{
    HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
    HIMC context = ImmGetContext(appWindow);
    int position = g_pfnImmGetCompositionStringW(context, GCS_CURSORPOS,
                                                 0, 0) & 0xffff;
    ImmReleaseContext(appWindow, context);
    return position;
}

static int __stdcall CopyCompositionString(DWORD flag, wchar_t *buffer,
                                           int byteCapacity)
{
    HWND appWindow = GetApplicationWindow();
    HIMC context = ImmGetContext(appWindow);
    int byteCount = g_pfnImmGetCompositionStringW(
        context, flag, buffer, byteCapacity - sizeof(wchar_t));
    ImmReleaseContext(appWindow, context);
    *reinterpret_cast<wchar_t *>(
        reinterpret_cast<unsigned char *>(buffer) + byteCount) = 0;
    return byteCount;
}

static HIMC __stdcall GetCurrentImeContext()
{
    return ImmGetContext(GetApplicationWindow());
}

static BOOL __stdcall ReleaseCurrentImeContext(HIMC context)
{
    return ImmReleaseContext(GetApplicationWindow(), context);
}

static HWND __stdcall GetDefaultImeWindow(HWND window)
{
    return ImmGetDefaultIMEWnd(window);
}

static HIMC __stdcall CreateImeContext()
{
    return ImmCreateContext();
}

static HIMC __stdcall AssociateImeContext(HWND window, HIMC context)
{
    return ImmAssociateContext(window, context);
}

static BOOL __stdcall SetImeOpenStatus(HIMC context, BOOL isOpen)
{
    return ImmSetOpenStatus(context, isOpen);
}

static DWORD __stdcall GetImeProperty(HKL layout, DWORD index)
{
    return ImmGetProperty(layout, index);
}

static BOOL __stdcall DestroyImeContext(HIMC context)
{
    return ImmDestroyContext(context);
}

static BOOL __stdcall GetImeConversionStatus(HIMC context,
                                              LPDWORD conversion,
                                              LPDWORD sentence)
{
    return ImmGetConversionStatus(context, conversion, sentence);
}

static BOOL __stdcall GetImeOpenStatus(HIMC context)
{
    return ImmGetOpenStatus(context);
}

static DWORD __stdcall GetImeCandidateList(HIMC context, DWORD index,
                                            LPCANDIDATELIST list,
                                            DWORD byteCount)
{
    return g_pfnImmGetCandidateListW(context, index, list, byteCount);
}

static LONG __stdcall GetImeCompositionString(HIMC context, DWORD index,
                                               LPVOID buffer,
                                               DWORD byteCount)
{
    return g_pfnImmGetCompositionStringW(context, index, buffer,
                                         byteCount);
}
```
